# Scientific data dictionary — `lunar_region_features.parquet`

One row per region (60). Every value is traceable in `lunar_observations.parquet` by (region_id, variable). Column-level provenance is also stored in the Parquet field metadata (`provenance`).

## Identity and geometry columns

| Column | Meaning | Unit |
|---|---|---|
| region_id | unique region identifier (L0_xx_CODE / L1_xx_CODE) | - |
| level | 0 = coarse (12 regions), 1 = fine (48 regions) | - |
| parent_region_id | L0 parent of an L1 region (empty for L0) | - |
| code, name | short code and descriptive name | - |
| lat_min, lat_max, lon_min, lon_max | region bounds (planetocentric, 0-360E) | degree |
| centroid_lat, centroid_lon | spherical area centroid | degree |
| area_km2, area_fraction | exact spherical area on R=1737.4 km; fraction of lunar surface | km2, - |
| is_polar_cap, hemisphere_ns, nearside_farside | geometric descriptors | - |
| qc_mean_coverage_<group> | mean coverage_fraction of that group's variables in the region | fraction |
| qc_n_variables_available | number of numeric variables with a value | count |
